subset=['Order Date', 'Survey ResponseID', 'ASIN/ISBN (Product Code)',\n            'Purchase Price Per Unit', 'Quantity'],\n    keep='first'\n)\n\n# Filter to 2019-2020 data only\npurchases_clean = purchases_clean[\n    (purchases_clean['Order Date'] >= '2019-01-01') &\n    (purchases_clean['Order Date'] <= '2020-12-31')\n]\n\nprint(f\"Cleaned purchase data shape: {purchases_clean.shape}\")\nprint(f\"Date range: {purchases_clean['Order Date'].min()} to {purchases_clean['Order Date'].max()}\")\nprint(f\"Unique categories: {purchases_clean['Category'].nunique()}\")\nprint(f\"Unique users: {purchases_clean['Survey ResponseID'].nunique()}\")\n\n# Save cleaned purchase data\npurchases_clean.to_csv(f'{OUTPUT_DIR}/amazon-purchases-2019-2020-filtered.csv', index=False)\nprint(f\"Cleaned purchase data saved\")\n\n# ============================================\n# STEP 3: DETECT CATEGORY ANOMALIES\n# ============================================\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"STEP 3: Detecting Category Anomalies\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\n\ndef calculate_anomaly_index(actual_mean, counterfactual_mean, scale=2.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate anomaly index ranging from -100 to 100\n    100 = maximum surge, 0 = normal, -100 = maximum slump\n    \"\"\"\n    if counterfactual_mean > 0:\n        relative_dev = (actual_mean - counterfactual_mean) / counterfactual_mean\n    else:\n        relative_dev = actual_mean if actual_mean != 0 else 0\n\n    # Apply tanh for bounded output\n    anomaly_index = np.tanh(relative_dev * scale) * 100\n    return anomaly_index\n\n# Aggregate daily sales by category\npurchases_clean['Date'] = purchases_clean['Order Date'].dt.date\ndaily_category_sales = purchases_clean.groupby(['Date', 'Category']).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': 'sum',\n    'Survey ResponseID': 'nunique',\n    'Quantity': 'sum'\n}).reset_index()\ndaily_category_sales.columns = ['Date', 'Category', 'Daily_Sales', 'N_Customers', 'N_Items']\ndaily_category_sales['Date'] = pd.to_datetime(daily_category_sales['Date'])\n\n# Calculate anomaly for each category\ncategory_anomalies = []\n\nfor category in daily_category_sales['Category'].unique():\n    cat_data = daily_category_sales[daily_category_sales['Category'] == category]\n\n    # Historical period (all data before March 2020)\n    historical = cat_data[\n        (cat_data['Date'] >= HISTORICAL_START) &\n        (cat_data['Date'] <= HISTORICAL_END)\n    ]\n\n    # Treatment period (March 2020)\n    treatment = cat_data[\n        (cat_data['Date'] >= TREATMENT_START) &\n        (cat_data['Date'] <= TREATMENT_END)\n    ]\n\n    if len(historical) < 7 or len(treatment) < 1:\n        continue\n\n    # Calculate counterfactual using historical average\n    # Weight more recent data higher\n    historical = historical.sort_values('Date')\n\n    # Use weighted average - more weight to recent months\n    historical['days_ago'] = (pd.to_datetime(HISTORICAL_END) - historical['Date']).dt.days\n    historical['weight'] = np.exp(-historical['days_ago'] / 90)  # 90-day half-life\n\n    counterfactual_daily = np.average(historical['Daily_Sales'], weights=historical['weight'])\n    actual_daily = treatment['Daily_Sales'].mean()\n\n    anomaly_idx = calculate_anomaly_index(actual_daily, counterfactual_daily)\n\n    category_anomalies.append({\n        'Category': category,\n        'Anomaly_Index': round(anomaly_idx, 2),\n        'Historical_Avg_Daily': round(counterfactual_daily, 2),\n        'Treatment_Avg_Daily': round(actual_daily, 2),\n        'N_Historical_Days': len(historical),\n        'N_Treatment_Days': len(treatment)\n    })\n\nanomaly_df = pd.DataFrame(category_anomalies)\nanomaly_df = anomaly_df.sort_values('Anomaly_Index', ascending=False)\n\n# Save anomaly index\nanomaly_output = anomaly_df[['Category', 'Anomaly_Index']].copy()\nanomaly_output.to_csv(f'{OUTPUT_DIR}/category_anomaly_index.csv', index=False)\n\nprint(f\"Analyzed {len(anomaly_df)} categories\")\nprint(\"\\nTop 10 Surge Categories:\")\nprint(anomaly_df.head(10)[['Category', 'Anomaly_Index', 'Historical_Avg_Daily', 'Treatment_Avg_Daily']])\nprint(\"\\nTop 10 Slump Categories:\")\nprint(anomaly_df.tail(10)[['Category', 'Anomaly_Index', 'Historical_Avg_Daily', 'Treatment_Avg_Daily']])\n\n# Get top 10 surge and slump categories\nsurge_categories = anomaly_df.head(10)['Category'].tolist()\nslump_categories = anomaly_df.tail(10)['Category'].tolist()\n\nprint(f\"\\nSurge categories: {surge_categories}\")\nprint(f\"Slump categories: {slump_categories}\")\n\n# ============================================\n# STEP 4: FEATURE ENGINEERING ON SURVEY DATA\n# ============================================\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"STEP 4: Feature Engineering on Survey Data\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\n\nsurvey_features = survey_clean.copy()\n\n# Keep Survey ResponseID for joining\nfeature_cols = ['Survey ResponseID']\n\n# 1. Age features\nage_map = {\n    '18 - 24 years': 21,\n    '25 - 34 years': 29.5,\n    '35 - 44 years': 39.5,\n    '45 - 54 years': 49.5,\n    '55 - 64 years': 59.5,\n    '65 and older': 70\n}\nsurvey_features['age_midpoint'] = survey_features['Q-demos-age'].map(age_map)\nsurvey_features['age_midpoint'] = survey_features['age_midpoint'].fillna(survey_features['age_midpoint'].median())\n\n# Age groups\nsurvey_features['is_young'] = (survey_features['Q-demos-age'].isin(['18 - 24 years', '25 - 34 years'])).astype(int)\nsurvey_features['is_middle_age'] = (survey_features['Q-demos-age'].isin(['35 - 44 years', '45 - 54 years'])).astype(int)\nsurvey_features['is_senior'] = (survey_features['Q-demos-age'].isin(['55 - 64 years', '65 and older'])).astype(int)\n\n# 2. Income features\nincome_map = {\n    'Less than $25,000': 12500,\n    '$25,000 - $49,999': 37500,\n    '$50,000 - $74,999': 62500,\n    '$75,000 - $99,999': 87500,\n    '$100,000 - $149,999': 125000,\n    '$150,000 or more': 175000,\n    'Prefer not to say': None\n}\nsurvey_features['income_midpoint'] = survey_features['Q-demos-income'].map(income_map)\nsurvey_features['income_midpoint'] = survey_features['income_midpoint'].fillna(survey_features['income_midpoint'].median())\n\n# Income brackets\nsurvey_features['is_low_income'] = (survey_features['Q-demos-income'] == 'Less than $25,000').astype(int)\nsurvey_features['is_middle_income'] = (survey_features['Q-demos-income'].isin(['$25,000 - $49,999', '$50,000 - $74,999'])).astype(int)\nsurvey_features['is_high_income'] = (survey_features['Q-demos-income'].isin(['$75,000 - $99,999', '$100,000 - $149,999', '$150,000 or more'])).astype(int)\n\n# 3. Gender features\nsurvey_features['is_female'] = (survey_features['Q-demos-gender'] == 'Female').astype(int)\nsurvey_features['is_male'] = (survey_features['Q-demos-gender'] == 'Male').astype(int)\n\n# 4. Hispanic ethnicity\nsurvey_features['is_hispanic'] = (survey_features['Q-demos-hispanic'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n# 5. Race features (one-hot encoding)\nrace_dummies = pd.get_dummies(survey_features['Q-demos-race'], prefix='race')\n# Simplify race columns\nsurvey_features['is_white'] = race_dummies.filter(like='White').max(axis=1) if any('White' in c for c in race_dummies.columns) else 0\nsurvey_features['is_black'] = race_dummies.filter(like='Black').max(axis=1) if any('Black' in c for c in race_dummies.columns) else 0\nsurvey_features['is_asian'] = race_dummies.filter(like='Asian').max(axis=1) if any('Asian' in c for c in race_dummies.columns) else 0\n\n# 6. Education features\nsurvey_features['has_college_degree'] = (survey_features['Q-demos-education'].isin([\n    \"Bachelor's degree\",\n    \"Graduate or professional degree (MA, MS, MBA, PhD, JD, MD, DDS, etc)\"\n])).astype(int)\nsurvey_features['has_graduate_degree'] = (survey_features['Q-demos-education'].str.contains(\n    'Graduate|professional', case=False, na=False\n)).astype(int)\nsurvey_features['is_high_school_only'] = (survey_features['Q-demos-education'].isin([\n    'High school diploma or GED', 'Some high school or less'\n])).astype(int)\n\n# 7. Household size features\nhh_map = {'1 (just me!)': 1, '2': 2, '3': 3, '4+': 4}\nsurvey_features['household_size'] = survey_features['Q-amazon-use-hh-size'].map(hh_map)\nsurvey_features['household_size'] = survey_features['household_size'].fillna(2)\n\nsurvey_features['is_single_household'] = (survey_features['household_size'] == 1).astype(int)\nsurvey_features['is_large_household'] = (survey_features['household_size'] >= 3).astype(int)\n\n# 8. Amazon usage features\nusers_map = {'1 (just me!)': 1, '2': 2, '3': 3, '4+': 4}\nsurvey_features['amazon_users'] = survey_features['Q-amazon-use-howmany'].map(users_map)\nsurvey_features['amazon_users'] = survey_features['amazon_users'].fillna(1)\n\n# Shopping frequency\nsurvey_features['is_heavy_shopper'] = (survey_features['Q-amazon-use-how-oft'] == 'More than 10 times per month').astype(int)\nsurvey_features['is_moderate_shopper'] = (survey_features['Q-amazon-use-how-oft'] == '5 - 10 times per month').astype(int)\nsurvey_features['is_light_shopper'] = (survey_features['Q-amazon-use-how-oft'] == 'Less than 5 times per month').astype(int)\n\n# 9. Substance use features\nsurvey_features['uses_cigarettes'] = (survey_features['Q-substance-use-cigarettes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\nsurvey_features['uses_marijuana'] = (survey_features['Q-substance-use-marijuana'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\nsurvey_features['uses_alcohol'] = (survey_features['Q-substance-use-alcohol'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n# 10. Health condition features\nsurvey_features['has_diabetes'] = (survey_features['Q-personal-diabetes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\nsurvey_features['uses_wheelchair'] = (survey_features['Q-personal-wheelchair'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n# 11. Sexual orientation\nsurvey_features['is_lgbtq'] = (survey_features['Q-sexual-orientation'] == 'lgbtq').astype(int)\n\n# 12. Geographic regions\nnortheast = ['Connecticut', 'Maine', 'Massachusetts', 'New Hampshire', 'Rhode Island',\n             'Vermont', 'New Jersey', 'New York', 'Pennsylvania']\nmidwest = ['Illinois', 'Indiana', 'Michigan', 'Ohio', 'Wisconsin', 'Iowa', 'Kansas',\n           'Minnesota', 'Missouri', 'Nebraska', 'North Dakota', 'South Dakota']\nsouth = ['Delaware', 'Florida', 'Georgia', 'Maryland', 'North Carolina', 'South Carolina',\n         'Virginia', 'West Virginia', 'Alabama', 'Kentucky', 'Mississippi', 'Tennessee',\n         'Arkansas', 'Louisiana', 'Oklahoma', 'Texas', 'Washington D.c.', 'District Of Columbia']\nwest = ['Arizona', 'Colorado', 'Idaho', 'Montana', 'Nevada', 'New Mexico', 'Utah',\n        'Wyoming', 'Alaska', 'California', 'Hawaii', 'Oregon', 'Washington']\n\nsurvey_features['region_northeast'] = survey_features['Q-demos-state'].isin(northeast).astype(int)\nsurvey_features['region_midwest'] = survey_features['Q-demos-state'].isin(midwest).astype(int)\nsurvey_features['region_south'] = survey_features['Q-demos-state'].isin(south).astype(int)\nsurvey_features['region_west'] = survey_features['Q-demos-state'].isin(west).astype(int)\n\n# Select engineered features\nengineered_cols = [\n    'Survey ResponseID',\n    'age_midpoint', 'is_young', 'is_middle_age', 'is_senior',\n    'income_midpoint', 'is_low_income', 'is_middle_income', 'is_high_income',\n    'is_female', 'is_male',\n    'is_hispanic', 'is_white', 'is_black', 'is_asian',\n    'has_college_degree', 'has_graduate_degree', 'is_high_school_only',\n    'household_size', 'is_single_household', 'is_large_household',\n    'amazon_users', 'is_heavy_shopper', 'is_moderate_shopper', 'is_light_shopper',\n    'uses_cigarettes', 'uses_marijuana', 'uses_alcohol',\n    'has_diabetes', 'uses_wheelchair', 'is_lgbtq',\n    'region_northeast', 'region_midwest', 'region_south', 'region_west'\n]\n\nsurvey_features_output = survey_features[engineered_cols].copy()\nsurvey_features_output.to_csv(f'{OUTPUT_DIR}/survey_feature_engineered.csv', index=False)\n\nprint(f\"Engineered {len(engineered_cols) - 1} features\")\nprint(f\"Features saved: {survey_features_output.shape}\")\n\n# ============================================\n# STEP 5: PREPARE DATA FOR CAUSAL ANALYSIS\n# ============================================\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"STEP 5: Preparing Data for Causal Analysis\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\n\n# Filter purchases to relevant categories\nrelevant_categories = surge_categories + slump_categories\nrelevant_purchases = purchases_clean[purchases_clean['Category'].isin(relevant_categories)].copy()\n\n# Add period indicator\nrelevant_purchases['Period'] = np.where(\n    (relevant_purchases['Order Date'] >= TREATMENT_START) &\n    (relevant_purchases['Order Date'] <= TREATMENT_END),\n    'treatment',\n    np.where(\n        (relevant_purchases['Order Date'] >= BASELINE_START) &\n        (relevant_purchases['Order Date'] <= BASELINE_END),\n        'baseline',\n        'other'\n    )\n)\n\n# Keep only baseline and treatment periods\nrelevant_purchases = relevant_purchases[relevant_purchases['Period'].isin(['baseline', 'treatment'])]\n\nprint(f\"Relevant purchases: {len(relevant_purchases)}\")\nprint(f\"Baseline: {(relevant_purchases['Period'] == 'baseline').sum()}\")\nprint(f\"Treatment: {(relevant_purchases['Period'] == 'treatment').sum()}\")\n\n# ============================================\n# INTENSIVE MARGIN DATA\n# ============================================\n# Aggregate by user, category, period\nintensive_data = relevant_purchases.groupby(\n    ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period']\n).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': 'sum'\n}).reset_index()\nintensive_data.columns = ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period', 'Total_Spend']\n\nintensive_data.to_csv(f'{OUTPUT_DIR}/user_category_period_aggregated_intensive.csv', index=False)\nprint(f\"Intensive margin data saved: {intensive_data.shape}\")\n\n# ============================================\n# EXTENSIVE MARGIN DATA\n# ============================================\n# For extensive margin, we need to include non-purchasers\n# Create a grid of all users x all categories x all periods\nall_users = survey_features_output['Survey ResponseID'].unique()\nall_categories = relevant_categories\nall_periods = ['baseline', 'treatment']\n\n# Create full grid\nfrom itertools import product\nfull_grid = pd.DataFrame(\n    list(product(all_users, all_categories, all_periods)),\n    columns=['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period']\n)\n\n# Get purchase indicators\npurchase_indicators = relevant_purchases.groupby(\n    ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period']\n).size().reset_index(name='n_purchases')\npurchase_indicators['Has_Purchase'] = 1\n\n# Merge to get has_purchase for all combinations\nextensive_data = full_grid.merge(\n    purchase_indicators[['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period', 'Has_Purchase']],\n    on=['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period'],\n    how='left'\n)\nextensive_data['Has_Purchase'] = extensive_data['Has_Purchase'].fillna(0).astype(int)\n\nextensive_data.to_csv(f'{OUTPUT_DIR}/user_category_period_aggregated_extensive.csv', index=False)\nprint(f\"Extensive margin data saved: {extensive_data.shape}\")\n\n# ============================================\n# STEP 6: CAUSAL ANALYSIS (DiD)\n# ============================================\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"STEP 6: Running Causal Analysis (DiD)\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\n\n# Merge with features\nintensive_with_features = intensive_data.merge(\n    survey_features_output, on='Survey ResponseID', how='inner'\n)\nextensive_with_features = extensive_data.merge(\n    survey_features_output, on='Survey ResponseID', how='inner'\n)\n\n# Feature columns for analysis (excluding ID columns)\nfeature_cols = [col for col in engineered_cols if col != 'Survey ResponseID']\nprint(f\"Analyzing {len(feature_cols)} demographic features\")\n\ndef run_univariate_did_intensive(df, feature_col, category):\n    \"\"\"Run univariate DiD for intensive margin\"\"\"\n    cat_data = df[df['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n    if len(cat_data) < 10:\n        return None\n\n    # Create post indicator\n    cat_data['post'] = (cat_data['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n    # Handle continuous features by binarizing at median\n    if cat_data[feature_col].nunique() > 2:\n        median_val = cat_data[feature_col].median()\n        cat_data['feature_high'] = (cat_data[feature_col] >= median_val).astype(int)\n    else:\n        cat_data['feature_high'] = cat_data[feature_col].astype(int)\n\n    # Create interaction\n    cat_data['post_x_feature'] = cat_data['post'] * cat_data['feature_high']\n\n    # Run regression\n    try:\n        X = sm.add_constant(cat_data[['post', 'feature_high', 'post_x_feature']])\n        y = cat_data['Total_Spend']\n        model = sm.OLS(y, X).fit(cov_type='HC3')\n\n        return {\n            'feature': feature_col,\n            'did_estimate': round(float(model.params.get('post_x_feature', 0)), 4),\n            'p_value': round(float(model.pvalues.get('post_x_feature', 1)), 4),\n            'method': 'Univariate DiD'\n        }\n    except:\n        return None\n\ndef run_univariate_did_extensive(df, feature_col, category):\n    \"\"\"Run univariate DiD for extensive margin\"\"\"\n    cat_data = df[df['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n    if len(cat_data) < 10:\n        return None\n\n    # Create post indicator\n    cat_data['post'] = (cat_data['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n    # Handle continuous features by binarizing at median\n    if cat_data[feature_col].nunique() > 2:\n        median_val = cat_data[feature_col].median()\n        cat_data['feature_high'] = (cat_data[feature_col] >= median_val).astype(int)\n    else:\n        cat_data['feature_high'] = cat_data[feature_col].astype(int)\n\n    # Create interaction\n    cat_data['post_x_feature'] = cat_data['post'] * cat_data['feature_high']\n\n    # Run regression (linear probability model for simplicity)\n    try:\n        X = sm.add_constant(cat_data[['post', 'feature_high', 'post_x_feature']])\n        y = cat_data['Has_Purchase']\n        model = sm.OLS(y, X).fit(cov_type='HC3')\n\n        return {\n            'feature': feature_col,\n            'did_estimate': round(float(model.params.get('post_x_feature', 0)), 4),\n            'p_value': round(float(model.pvalues.get('post_x_feature', 1)), 4),\n            'method': 'Multivariate Heterogeneous DiD'\n        }\n    except:\n        return None\n\ndef analyze_category(category, is_surge=True):\n    \"\"\"Analyze a single category for intensive and extensive margin drivers\"\"\"\n\n    # Get anomaly info\n    cat_anomaly = anomaly_df[anomaly_df['Category'] == category].iloc[0]\n\n    # Calculate category-level statistics\n    cat_purchases = relevant_purchases[relevant_purchases['Category'] == category]\n\n    baseline_purchases = cat_purchases[cat_purchases['Period'] == 'baseline']\n    treatment_purchases = cat_purchases[cat_purchases['Period'] == 'treatment']\n\n    baseline_avg_spend = baseline_purchases.groupby('Survey ResponseID')['Total_Amount'].sum().mean() if len(baseline_purchases) > 0 else 0\n    treatment_avg_spend = treatment_purchases.groupby('Survey ResponseID')['Total_Amount'].sum().mean() if len(treatment_purchases) > 0 else 0\n\n    n_purchasers_baseline = baseline_purchases['Survey ResponseID'].nunique()\n    n_purchasers_treatment = treatment_purchases['Survey ResponseID'].nunique()\n\n    n_at_risk = len(all_users)\n    baseline_purchase_rate = n_purchasers_baseline / n_at_risk if n_at_risk > 0 else 0\n    treatment_purchase_rate = n_purchasers_treatment / n_at_risk if n_at_risk > 0 else 0\n\n    # Run DiD for each feature\n    intensive_results = []\n    extensive_results = []\n\n    for feature in feature_cols:\n        # Intensive margin\n        result = run_univariate_did_intensive(intensive_with_features, feature, category)\n        if result:\n            intensive_results.append(result)\n\n        # Extensive margin\n        result = run_univariate_did_extensive(extensive_with_features, feature, category)\n        if result:\n            extensive_results.append(result)\n\n    # Sort by contribution strength\n    if is_surge:\n        # For surge: sort by did_estimate descending (largest positives first)\n        intensive_results.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n        extensive_results.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n    else:\n        # For slump: sort by did_estimate ascending (largest negatives first)\n        intensive_results.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=False)\n        extensive_results.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=False)\n\n    return {\n        'category': category,\n        'anomaly_index': round(float(cat_anomaly['Anomaly_Index']), 2),\n        'baseline_avg_spend': round(float(baseline_avg_spend), 2),\n        'treatment_avg_spend': round(float(treatment_avg_spend), 2),\n        'n_purchasers_baseline': int(n_purchasers_baseline),\n        'n_purchasers_treatment': int(n_purchasers_treatment),\n        'baseline_purchase_rate': round(float(baseline_purchase_rate), 4),\n        'treatment_purchase_rate': round(float(treatment_purchase_rate), 4),\n        'n_at_risk': int(n_at_risk),\n        'intensive_margin': intensive_results[:3],  # Top 3 drivers\n        'extensive_margin': extensive_results[:3]   # Top 3 drivers\n    }\n\n# Analyze all categories\nprint(\"\\nAnalyzing surge categories...\")\nsurge_results = []\nfor cat in surge_categories:\n    print(f\"  Analyzing {cat}...\")\n    result = analyze_category(cat, is_surge=True)\n    surge_results.append(result)\n\nprint(\"\\nAnalyzing slump categories...\")\nslump_results = []\nfor cat in slump_categories:\n    print(f\"  Analyzing {cat}...\")\n    result = analyze_category(cat, is_surge=False)\n    slump_results.append(result)\n\n# Build final report\nreport = {\n    'metadata': {\n        'baseline_start': datetime.strptime(BASELINE_START, '%Y-%m-%d').strftime('%m-%d-%Y'),\n        'baseline_end': datetime.strptime(BASELINE_END, '%Y-%m-%d').strftime('%m-%d-%Y'),\n        'treatment_start': datetime.strptime(TREATMENT_START, '%Y-%m-%d').strftime('%m-%d-%Y'),\n        'treatment_end': datetime.strptime(TREATMENT_END, '%Y-%m-%d').strftime('%m-%d-%Y'),\n        'total_features_analyzed': len(feature_cols)\n    },\n    'surge_categories': surge_results,\n    'slump_categories': slump_results,\n    'summary': {\n        'surge': {\n            'total_categories': len(surge_results),\n            'total_intensive_drivers': sum(len(r['intensive_margin']) for r in surge_results),\n            'total_extensive_drivers': sum(len(r['extensive_margin']) for r in surge_results)\n        },\n        'slump': {\n            'total_categories': len(slump_results),\n            'total_intensive_drivers': sum(len(r['intensive_margin']) for r in slump_results),\n            'total_extensive_drivers': sum(len(r['extensive_margin']) for r in slump_results)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n# Save report\nwith open(f'{OUTPUT_DIR}/causal_analysis_report.json', 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"ANALYSIS COMPLETE\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\nprint(f\"\\nOutput files saved to {OUTPUT_DIR}:\")\nprint(\"  1. survey_cleaned.csv\")\nprint(\"  2. amazon-purchases-2019-2020-filtered.csv\")\nprint(\"  3. category_anomaly_index.csv\")\nprint(\"  4. survey_feature_engineered.csv\")\nprint(\"  5. user_category_period_aggregated_intensive.csv\")\nprint(\"  6. user_category_period_aggregated_extensive.csv\")\nprint(\"  7. causal_analysis_report.json\")\n\nprint(f\"\\nSummary:\")\nprint(f\"  - \"\"\"\n    if counterfactual_mean > 0:\n        relative_dev = (actual_mean - counterfactual_mean) / counterfactual_mean\n    else:\n        relative_dev = actual_mean if actual_mean != 0 else 0\n\n 90)  # 90-day half-life\n\n    counterfactual_daily = np.average(historical['Daily_Sales'], weights=historical['weight'])\n    actual_daily = treatment['Daily_Sales'].mean()\n\n    anomaly_idx = Education features\nsurvey_features['has_college_degree'] = (survey_features['Q-demos-education'].isin([\n    \"Bachelor's degree\",\n    \"Graduate or professional degree (MA, MS, MBA, PhD, JD, MD, DDS, etc)\"\n])).astype(int)\nsurvey_features['has_graduate_degree'] = (survey_features['Q-demos-education'].str.contains(\n    'Graduate|professional', case=False, na=False\n)).astype(int)\nsurvey_features['is_high_school_only'] = (survey_features['Q-demos-education'].isin([\n    'High school diploma or GED', 'Some high school or less'\n])).astype(int)\n\n# 7. Household size features\nhh_map = {'1 (just me!)': 1, '2': 2, '3': 3, '4+': 4}\nsurvey_features['household_size'] = survey_features['Q-amazon-use-hh-size'].map(hh_map)\nsurvey_features['household_size'] = survey_features['household_size'].fillna(2)\n\nsurvey_features['is_single_household'] = (survey_features['household_size'] == 1).astype(int)\nsurvey_features['is_large_household'] = (survey_features['household_size'] >= 3).astype(int)\n\n# 8. Amazon usage features\nusers_map = {'1 (just me!)': 1, '2': 2, '3': 3, '4+': 4}\nsurvey_features['amazon_users'] = survey_features['Q-amazon-use-howmany'].map(users_map)\nsurvey_features['amazon_users'] = survey_features['amazon_users'].fillna(1)\n\n# Shopping frequency\nsurvey_features['is_heavy_shopper'] = (survey_features['Q-amazon-use-how-oft'] == 'More than 10 times per month').astype(int)\nsurvey_features['is_moderate_shopper'] = (survey_features['Q-amazon-use-how-oft'] == '5 - 10 times per month').astype(int)\nsurvey_features['is_light_shopper'] = (survey_features['Q-amazon-use-how-oft'] == 'Less than 5 times per month').astype(int)\n\n# 9. Health condition features\nsurvey_features['has_diabetes'] = (survey_features['Q-personal-diabetes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\nsurvey_features['uses_wheelchair'] = (survey_features['Q-personal-wheelchair'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n# 11. Sexual orientation\nsurvey_features['is_lgbtq'] = (survey_features['Q-sexual-orientation'] == 'lgbtq').astype(int)\n\n# 12. ============================================\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"STEP 5: Preparing Data for Causal Analysis\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\n\n# Filter purchases to relevant np.where(\n        (relevant_purchases['Order Date'] >= BASELINE_START) &\n        (relevant_purchases['Order Date'] <= BASELINE_END),\n        'baseline',\n        'other'\n    )\n)\n\n# Keep only ============================================\n# INTENSIVE MARGIN DATA\n# ============================================\n# Aggregate by user, category, period\nintensive_data = relevant_purchases.groupby(\n    ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period']\n).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': 'sum'\n}).reset_index()\nintensive_data.columns = ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period', 'Total_Spend']\n\nintensive_data.to_csv(f'{OUTPUT_DIR}/user_category_period_aggregated_intensive.csv', index=False)\nprint(f\"Intensive margin data saved: {intensive_data.shape}\")\n\n# ============================================\n# EXTENSIVE MARGIN DATA\n# ============================================\n# For extensive margin, we need to include non-purchasers\n# Create a grid of all users x all categories x all periods\nall_users = survey_features_output['Survey ResponseID'].unique()\nall_categories = relevant_categories\nall_periods = ['baseline', 'treatment']\n\n# Create full grid\nfrom itertools import product\nfull_grid = pd.DataFrame(\n    list(product(all_users, all_categories, all_periods)),\n    columns=['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period']\n)\n\n# Get purchase indicators\npurchase_indicators = relevant_purchases.groupby(\n    ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period']\n).size().reset_index(name='n_purchases')\npurchase_indicators['Has_Purchase'] = 1\n\n# Merge to get has_purchase for all combinations\nextensive_data = full_grid.merge(\n    purchase_indicators[['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period', 'Has_Purchase']],\n    on=['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period'],\n    how='left'\n)\nextensive_data['Has_Purchase'] = extensive_data['Has_Purchase'].fillna(0).astype(int)\n\nextensive_data.to_csv(f'{OUTPUT_DIR}/user_category_period_aggregated_extensive.csv', index=False)\nprint(f\"Extensive margin data saved: {extensive_data.shape}\")\n\n# ============================================\n# STEP 6: CAUSAL ANALYSIS (DiD)\n# ============================================\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"STEP 6: Running Causal Analysis (DiD)\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\n\n# Merge with features\nintensive_with_features = intensive_data.merge(\n    survey_features_output, on='Survey ResponseID', feature_col, category):\n    \"\"\"Run univariate DiD for intensive margin\"\"\"\n    cat_data = df[df['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n    if len(cat_data) < 10:\n        return None\n\n    # Create post indicator\n    cat_data['post'] = (cat_data['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n    # Handle continuous features by binarizing at median\n    if cat_data[feature_col].nunique() > 2:\n        median_val = cat_data[feature_col].median()\n        cat_data['feature_high'] = (cat_data[feature_col] >= median_val).astype(int)\n    else:\n        cat_data['feature_high'] = cat_data[feature_col].astype(int)\n\n    # Create interaction\n    cat_data['post_x_feature'] = cat_data['post'] * cat_data['feature_high']\n\n    # Run regression\n    try:\n        X = sm.add_constant(cat_data[['post', 'feature_high', 'post_x_feature']])\n        y = cat_data['Total_Spend']\n        model = sm.OLS(y, X).fit(cov_type='HC3')\n\n        return {\n            'feature': feature_col,\n            'did_estimate': round(float(model.params.get('post_x_feature', 0)), 4),\n            'p_value': round(float(model.pvalues.get('post_x_feature', 1)), 4),\n            'method': 'Univariate DiD'\n        }\n    except:\n        return None\n\ndef run_univariate_did_extensive(df, feature_col, category):\n    \"\"\"Run univariate DiD for extensive margin\"\"\"\n    cat_data = df[df['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n    if len(cat_data) < 10:\n        return None\n\n    # Create post indicator\n    cat_data['post'] = (cat_data['Period'] == cat_data['feature_high'] = (cat_data[feature_col] >= median_val).astype(int)\n    else:\n        cat_data['feature_high'] = cat_data[feature_col].astype(int)\n\n    # Create interaction\n    cat_data['post_x_feature'] = cat_data['post'] * cat_data['feature_high']\n\n    # Run regression (linear probability model for simplicity)\n    try:\n        X = sm.add_constant(cat_data[['post', 'feature_high', 'post_x_feature']])\n        y = cat_data['Has_Purchase']\n        model = sm.OLS(y, X).fit(cov_type='HC3')\n\n        return {\n            'feature': feature_col,\n            'did_estimate': round(float(model.params.get('post_x_feature', 0)), 4),\n            'p_value': round(float(model.pvalues.get('post_x_feature', 1)), 4),\n            'method': 'Multivariate Heterogeneous DiD'\n        }\n    except:\n        return None\n\ndef analyze_category(category, is_surge=True):\n    \"\"\"Analyze a single category for intensive and extensive margin drivers\"\"\"\n\n    # Get anomaly info\n    cat_anomaly = anomaly_df[anomaly_df['Category'] == category].iloc[0]\n\n    # Calculate category-level statistics\n    cat_purchases = relevant_purchases[relevant_purchases['Category'] == category]\n\n    baseline_purchases = cat_purchases[cat_purchases['Period'] == 'baseline']\n    treatment_purchases = cat_purchases[cat_purchases['Period'] == 'treatment']\n\n    baseline_avg_spend = baseline_purchases.groupby('Survey ResponseID')['Total_Amount'].sum().mean() if len(baseline_purchases) > 0 else 0\n    treatment_avg_spend = treatment_purchases.groupby('Survey ResponseID')['Total_Amount'].sum().mean() if len(treatment_purchases) > 0 else 0\n\n    n_purchasers_baseline = baseline_purchases['Survey ResponseID'].nunique()\n    n_purchasers_treatment = treatment_purchases['Survey ResponseID'].nunique()\n\n    n_at_risk = len(all_users)\n    baseline_purchase_rate = n_purchasers_baseline / n_at_risk if n_at_risk > 0 else 0\n    treatment_purchase_rate = n_purchasers_treatment / n_at_risk if n_at_risk > 0 else 0\n\n    # Run DiD for each feature\n    intensive_results = []\n    extensive_results = []\n\n    for feature in feature_cols:\n        # Intensive margin\n        result = run_univariate_did_intensive(intensive_with_features, feature, category)\n        if result:\n            intensive_results.append(result)\n\n        # Extensive margin\n        result = run_univariate_did_extensive(extensive_with_features, feature, category)\n        if result:\n            extensive_results.append(result)\n\n    # Sort by contribution strength\n    if is_surge:\n        # For surge: sort by did_estimate descending (largest positives first)\n        intensive_results.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n        extensive_results.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n    else:\n        # For slump: sort by did_estimate ascending (largest negatives first)\n        intensive_results.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=False)\n        extensive_results.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=False)\n\n    return {\n        'category': category,\n        'anomaly_index': round(float(cat_anomaly['Anomaly_Index']), 2),\n        'baseline_avg_spend': round(float(baseline_avg_spend), 2),\n        'treatment_avg_spend': round(float(treatment_avg_spend), 2),\n        'n_purchasers_baseline': int(n_purchasers_baseline),\n        'n_purchasers_treatment': int(n_purchasers_treatment),\n        'baseline_purchase_rate': round(float(baseline_purchase_rate), 4),\n        'treatment_purchase_rate': round(float(treatment_purchase_rate), 4),\n        'n_at_risk': int(n_at_risk),\n        'intensive_margin': intensive_results[:3],  # Top 3 drivers\n        'extensive_margin': extensive_results[:3]   # Top 3 drivers\n    }\n\n# Analyze all categories\nprint(\"\\nAnalyzing surge categories...\")\nsurge_results = []\nfor cat in surge_categories:\n    print(f\"  Analyzing {cat}...\")\n    result = analyze_category(cat, is_surge=True)\n    surge_results.append(result)\n\nprint(\"\\nAnalyzing slump categories...\")\nslump_results = []\nfor cat in slump_categories:\n    print(f\"  Analyzing {cat}...\")\n    result = analyze_category(cat, is_surge=False)\n    slump_results.append(result)\n\n# Build final report\nreport = {\n    'metadata': {\n        'baseline_start': datetime.strptime(BASELINE_START, '%Y-%m-%d').strftime('%m-%d-%Y'),\n        'baseline_end': datetime.strptime(BASELINE_END, '%Y-%m-%d').strftime('%m-%d-%Y'),\n        'treatment_start': datetime.strptime(TREATMENT_START, '%Y-%m-%d').strftime('%m-%d-%Y'),\n        'treatment_end': datetime.strptime(TREATMENT_END, '%Y-%m-%d').strftime('%m-%d-%Y'),\n        'total_features_analyzed': len(feature_cols)\n    },\n calculate_anomaly_index(actual_mean, counterfactual_mean, scale=2.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate anomaly index ranging from -100 to 100\n    100 = maximum surge, 0 = normal, -100 = maximum slump\n    \"\"\"\n    if counterfactual_mean > 0:\n        relative_dev = (actual_mean - counterfactual_mean) / counterfactual_mean\n    else:\n        relative_dev = actual_mean if actual_mean != 0 else 0\n\n    # Apply tanh for bounded output\n    anomaly_index = np.tanh(relative_dev * scale) * 100\n    return anomaly_index\n\n# Aggregate daily sales by category\npurchases_clean['Date'] = 90)  # 90-day half-life\n\n    counterfactual_daily = np.average(historical['Daily_Sales'], weights=historical['weight'])\n    actual_daily = treatment['Daily_Sales'].mean()\n\n    anomaly_idx = calculate_anomaly_index(actual_daily, counterfactual_daily)\n\n    category_anomalies.append({\n        'Category': category,\n        'Anomaly_Index': round(anomaly_idx, 2),\n        'Historical_Avg_Daily': round(counterfactual_daily, 2),\n        'Treatment_Avg_Daily': round(actual_daily, 2),\n        'N_Historical_Days': len(historical),\n        'N_Treatment_Days': feature_col, category):\n    \"\"\"Run univariate DiD for intensive margin\"\"\"\n    cat_data = df[df['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n    if len(cat_data) < 10:\n        return None\n\n    # Create post indicator\n    cat_data['post'] = (cat_data['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n    # Always binarize features at median (even for continuous ones)\n    # This ensures interpretable DiD coefficients\n    if cat_data[feature_col].nunique() > 2:\n        median_val = cat_data[feature_col].median()\n        cat_data['feature_high'] = (cat_data[feature_col] >= median_val).astype(int)\n    else:\n        cat_data['feature_high'] = cat_data[feature_col].astype(int)\n\n    # Check we have variation in both dimensions\n    if cat_data['feature_high'].nunique() < 2:\n        return None\n    if cat_data.groupby(['post', 'feature_high']).size().shape[0] < 3:\n        return None\n\n    # Create interaction\n    cat_data['post_x_feature'] = cat_data['post'] * sm.OLS(y, X).fit(cov_type='HC3')\n\n        did_est = float(model.params.get('post_x_feature', 0))\n        p_val = float(model.pvalues.get('post_x_feature', 1))\n\n        # Sanity check - DiD estimate should be reasonable (not larger than 10x the max spend)\n        max_spend = cat_data['Total_Spend'].max()\n        if abs(did_est) > max_spend * 10:\n            return None\n\n        return {\n            'feature': feature_col,\n            'did_estimate': round(did_est, 4),\n            'p_value': round(p_val, 4),\n            'method': 'Univariate DiD'\n        }\n    except:\n        return None\n\ndef run_univariate_did_extensive(df, feature_col, category):\n    \"\"\"Run univariate DiD for extensive margin\"\"\"\n    cat_data = df[df['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n n_at_risk if n_at_risk > 0 else 0\n\n    # Run DiD for each feature\n    intensive_results = []\n    extensive_results = []\n\n    for feature in feature_cols:\n        # Intensive margin\n  'treatment_purchase_rate': round(float(treatment_purchase_rate), 4),\n        'n_at_risk': int(n_at_risk),\n        'intensive_margin': intensive_results[:3],  # Top 3 drivers\n        'extensive_margin': extensive_results[:3]   # Top 3 drivers\n    }\n\n# Analyze all categories\nprint(\"\\nAnalyzing surge categories...\")\nsurge_results = []\nfor cat in surge_categories:\n
